#+TITLE: Inventory of every binding
#+STARTUP: overview

Every command bound in every keymap this configuration reaches, generated from
the live keymaps. Refreshed by =C-c k b= (or =C-c C-c= on the block).

This is the completeness document: if a key works somewhere in your Emacs, it is
in here. The curated guides are =cheatsheet.org= (what to use day to day) and
=HANDBOOK.org= (the built-in subsystems, and when to reach for them).

Two caveats, stated rather than hidden:

- Keymaps of packages that are not loaded yet cannot be walked. The block
  requires the packages it knows about; anything that fails is listed at the
  bottom instead of being silently dropped.
- Only *bound* commands appear. Emacs ships about 5,100 interactive commands;
  most are reachable only through =M-x= and are not listed here.

#+BEGIN: my-inventory
4430 bindings across 32 keymaps. Generated from the live keymaps.

| =C-x v O= | =vc-root-log-outgoing= | Show log of changes that will be sent with a push to UPSTREAM-LOCATION |
| =C-x v P= | =vc-push= | Push the current branch |
| =C-x v R= | =vc-rename-file= | Rename file OLD to NEW in both working tree and repository |
| =C-x v T == | =vc-diff-unintegrated= | Report changes to VC fileset since the merge base with UPSTREAM-LOCATION |
| =C-x v T D= | =vc-root-diff-unintegrated= | Report diff of all changes since the merge base with UPSTREAM-LOCATION |
| =C-x v T L= | =vc-root-log-unintegrated= | Show log of revisions since the merge base with UPSTREAM-LOCATION |
| =C-x v T R == | =vc-diff-remote-unintegrated= | Show remote fileset changes since merge base with UPSTREAM-LOCATION |
| =C-x v T R D= | =vc-root-diff-remote-unintegrated= | Report diff of remote changes since merge base with UPSTREAM-LOCATION |
| =C-x v T R L= | =vc-root-log-remote-unintegrated= | Show log of remote revisions since merge base with UPSTREAM-LOCATION |
| =C-x v T R l= | =vc-log-remote-unintegrated= | Show remote log for VC fileset since merge base with UPSTREAM-LOCATION |
| =C-x v T l= | =vc-log-unintegrated= | Show log for the VC fileset since the merge base with UPSTREAM-LOCATION |
| =C-x v a= | =vc-update-change-log= | Find change log file and add entries from recent version control logs |
| =C-x v b L= | =vc-print-root-branch-log= | Show root log of VC changes on BRANCH in another window |
| =C-x v b c= | =vc-create-branch= | Make a branch called NAME in directory DIR |
| =C-x v b l= | =vc-print-fileset-branch-log= | Show log of VC changes on BRANCH, limited to the current fileset |
| =C-x v b s= | =vc-switch-branch= | Switch to the branch NAME in the directory DIR |
| =C-x v d= | =vc-dir= | Show the VC status for "interesting" files in and below DIR |
| =C-x v g= | =vc-annotate= | Display the edit history of the current FILE using colors |
| =C-x v h= | =vc-region-history= | Show the history of the region between FROM and TO |
| =C-x v i= | =vc-register= | Register into a version control system |
| =C-x v l= | =vc-print-log= | Show in another window the VC change history of the current fileset |
| =C-x v m= | =vc-merge= | Perform a version control merge operation |
| =C-x v r= | =vc-retrieve-tag= | For each file in or below DIR, retrieve their version identified by tag NAME |
| =C-x v s= | =vc-create-tag= | Descending recursively from DIR, make a tag called NAME |
| =C-x v u= | =vc-revert= | Revert working copies of the selected fileset to their repository contents |
| =C-x v v= | =vc-next-action= | Do the next logical version control operation on the current fileset |
| =C-x v w A= | =vc-apply-root-to-other-working-tree= | Apply all local changes in this working tree to the tree under DIRECTORY |
| =C-x v w R= | =vc-move-working-tree= | Relocate a working tree from FROM to TO, two directory file names |
| =C-x v w a= | =vc-apply-to-other-working-tree= | Apply VC fileset's local changes to working tree under DIRECTORY |
| =C-x v w c= | =vc-add-working-tree= | Create working tree DIRECTORY with same backing repository as this tree |
| =C-x v w k= | =vc-kill-other-working-tree-buffers= | Kill buffers visiting versions of this file in other working trees |
| =C-x v w s= | =vc-working-tree-switch-project= | Like C-c p p but limited to projects with the same backing repository |
| =C-x v w w= | =vc-switch-working-tree= | Switch to the version of this file in working tree under DIRECTORY |
| =C-x v w x= | =vc-delete-working-tree= | Delete working tree DIRECTORY with same backing repository as this tree |
| =C-x v x= | =vc-delete-file= | Delete file and mark it as such in the version control system |
| =C-x v ~= | =vc-revision-other-window= | Visit revision REV of the current file in another window |
| =C-x w -= | =fit-window-to-buffer= | Adjust size of WINDOW to display its buffer's contents exactly |
| =C-x w 0= | =delete-windows-on= | Delete all windows showing BUFFER-OR-NAME |
| =C-x w 2= | =split-root-window-below= | Split root window of current frame in two |
| =C-x w 3= | =split-root-window-right= | Split root window of current frame into two side-by-side windows |
| =C-x w ^ f= | =tear-off-window= | Delete the selected window, and create a new frame displaying its buffer |
| =C-x w ^ t= | =tab-window-detach= | Move the selected window to a new tab |
| =C-x w d= | =toggle-window-dedicated= | Toggle whether WINDOW is dedicated to its current buffer |
| =C-x w f <down>= | =window-layout-flip-topdown= | Flip WINDOW's child windows vertically |
| =C-x w f <left>= | =window-layout-flip-leftright= | Flip WINDOW's child windows horizontally |
| =C-x w f <right>= | =window-layout-flip-leftright= | Flip WINDOW's child windows horizontally |
| =C-x w f <up>= | =window-layout-flip-topdown= | Flip WINDOW's child windows vertically |
| =C-x w o <left>= | =rotate-windows-back= | Rotate child windows of WINDOW backwards in cyclic ordering |
| =C-x w o <right>= | =rotate-windows= | Rotate child windows of WINDOW in cyclic ordering |
| =C-x w q= | =quit-window= | Quit WINDOW and bury its buffer |
| =C-x w r <left>= | =window-layout-rotate-anticlockwise= | Rotate layout of WINDOW's child windows counterclockwise by 90 degrees |
| =C-x w r <right>= | =window-layout-rotate-clockwise= | Rotate layout of WINDOW's child windows clockwise by 90 degrees |
| =C-x w s= | =window-toggle-side-windows= | Toggle display of side windows on specified FRAME |
| =C-x w t= | =window-layout-transpose= | Transpose child windows of WINDOW |
| =C-x x @= | =tramp-revert-buffer-with-sudo= | Visit the current file again with superuser, or root, permissions |
| =C-x x f= | =font-lock-update= | Update the syntax highlighting in this buffer |
| =C-x x g= | =revert-buffer-quick= | Like 'revert-buffer', but asks for less confirmation |
| =C-x x i= | =insert-buffer= | Insert after point the contents of BUFFER |
| =C-x x n= | =clone-buffer= | Create and return a twin copy of the current buffer |
| =C-x x r= | =rename-buffer= | Change current buffer's name to NEWNAME (a string) |
| =C-x x t= | =toggle-truncate-lines= | Toggle truncating of long lines for the current buffer |
| =C-x x u= | =rename-uniquely= | Rename current buffer to a similar name not already taken |
| =C-x z= | =repeat= | Repeat most recently executed command |

** Org mode (301)
| Key | Command | What it does |
|---+---+---|
| =<backtab>= | =outline-cycle-buffer= | Cycle visibility state of the body lines of the whole buffer |
| =<backtab>= | =outline-cycle-buffer= | Cycle visibility state of the body lines of the whole buffer |
| =<backtab>= | =org-shifttab= | Global visibility cycling or move to previous table field |
| =<remap> <backward-paragraph>= | =org-backward-paragraph= | Move backward by a paragraph, or equivalent, unit |
| =<remap> <backward-sentence>= | =org-backward-sentence= | Go to beginning of sentence, or beginning of table field |
| =<remap> <comment-dwim>= | =org-comment-dwim= | Call the comment command you mean |
| =<remap> <delete-backward-char>= | =org-delete-backward-char= | Like 'delete-backward-char', insert whitespace at field end in tables |
| =<remap> <delete-char>= | =org-delete-char= | Like 'delete-char', but insert whitespace at field end in tables |
| =<remap> <delete-indentation>= | =org-delete-indentation= | Join current line to previous and fix whitespace at join |
| =<remap> <fill-paragraph>= | =org-fill-paragraph= | Fill element at point, when applicable |
| =<remap> <forward-paragraph>= | =org-forward-paragraph= | Move forward by a paragraph, or equivalent, unit |
| =<remap> <forward-sentence>= | =org-forward-sentence= | Go to end of sentence, or end of table field |
| =<remap> <kill-line>= | =org-kill-line= | Kill line, to tags or end of line |
| =<remap> <move-beginning-of-line>= | =org-beginning-of-line= | Go to the beginning of the current visible line |
| =<remap> <move-end-of-line>= | =org-end-of-line= | Go to the end of the line, but before ellipsis, if any |
| =<remap> <open-line>= | =org-open-line= | Insert a new row in tables, call 'open-line' elsewhere |
| =<remap> <outline-backward-same-level>= | =org-backward-heading-same-level= | Move backward to the ARG'th subheading at same level as this one |
| =<remap> <outline-demote>= | =org-demote-subtree= | Demote the entire subtree |
| =<remap> <outline-forward-same-level>= | =org-forward-heading-same-level= | Move forward to the ARG'th subheading at same level as this one |
| =<remap> <outline-insert-heading>= | =org-ctrl-c-ret= | Call 'org-table-hline-and-move' or 'org-insert-heading'. |
| =<remap> <outline-mark-subtree>= | =org-mark-subtree= | Mark the current subtree |
| =<remap> <outline-next-visible-heading>= | =org-next-visible-heading= | Move to the next visible heading line |
| =<remap> <outline-previous-visible-heading>= | =org-previous-visible-heading= | Move to the previous visible heading |
| =<remap> <outline-promote>= | =org-promote-subtree= | Promote the entire subtree |
| =<remap> <outline-show-branches>= | =org-kill-note-or-show-branches= | Abort storing current note, or show just branches. |
| =<remap> <outline-show-children>= | =org-fold-show-children= | Show all direct subheadings of this heading |
| =<remap> <outline-show-subtree>= | =org-fold-show-subtree= | Show everything after this heading at deeper levels. |
| =<remap> <outline-up-heading>= | =org-up-heading= | Move to the visible heading line of which the present line is a subheading |
| =<remap> <self-insert-command>= | =org-self-insert-command= | Like 'self-insert-command', use 'overwrite-mode' for whitespace in tables |
| =<remap> <transpose-words>= | =org-transpose-words= | Transpose words for Org |
| =<remap> <yank>= | =org-yank= | Yank |
| =C-#= | =org-table-rotate-recalc-marks= | Rotate the recalculation mark in the first column |
| =C-'= | =org-cycle-agenda-files= | Cycle through the files in 'org-agenda-files' |
| =C-,= | =org-cycle-agenda-files= | Cycle through the files in 'org-agenda-files' |
| =C-<return>= | =org-insert-heading-respect-content= | Insert heading with 'org-insert-heading-respect-content' set to t |
| =C-M-S-<left>= | =org-decrease-number-at-point= | Decrement the number at point |
| =C-M-S-<right>= | =org-increase-number-at-point= | Increment the number at point |
| =C-M-i= | =nil= |  |
| =C-M-t= | =org-transpose-element= | Transpose current and previous elements, keeping blank lines between |
| =C-S-<down>= | =org-shiftcontroldown= | Change timestamps synchronously down in CLOCK log lines |
| =C-S-<left>= | =org-shiftcontrolleft= | Switch to previous TODO set. |
| =C-S-<return>= | =org-insert-todo-heading-respect-content= | Call 'org-insert-todo-heading', inserting after current subtree |
| =C-S-<right>= | =org-shiftcontrolright= | Switch to next TODO set. |
| =C-S-<up>= | =org-shiftcontrolup= | Change timestamps synchronously up in CLOCK log lines |
| =C-c != | =org-timestamp-inactive= | Insert an inactive time stamp |
| =C-c " a= | =orgtbl-ascii-plot= | Draw an ASCII bar plot in a column |
| =C-c " g= | =org-plot/gnuplot= | Plot table using gnuplot |
| =C-c #= | =org-update-statistics-cookies= | Update the statistics cookie, either from TODO or from checkboxes |
| =C-c $= | =org-archive-subtree= | Move the current subtree to the archive |
| =C-c %= | =org-mark-ring-push= | Put the current position into the mark ring and rotate it |
| =C-c &= | =org-mark-ring-goto= | Jump to the previous position in the mark ring |
| =C-c '= | =org-edit-special= | Call a special editor for the element at point |
| =C-c *= | =org-ctrl-c-star= | Compute table, or change heading status of lines |
| =C-c += | =org-table-sum= | Sum numbers in region of current table column |

** Outline (25)
| Key | Command | What it does |
|---+---+---|
| =<backtab>= | =outline-cycle-buffer= | Cycle visibility state of the body lines of the whole buffer |
| =C-c / h= | =outline-hide-by-heading-regexp= | Hide outlines whose headings match REGEXP |
| =C-c / s= | =outline-show-by-heading-regexp= | Show outlines whose headings match REGEXP |
| =C-c @= | =outline-mark-subtree= | Mark the current subtree in an outlined document |
| =C-c C-<= | =outline-promote= | Promote headings higher up the tree |
| =C-c C->= | =outline-demote= | Demote headings lower down the tree |
| =C-c C-^= | =outline-move-subtree-up= | Move the current subtree up past ARG headlines of the same level |
| =C-c C-a= | =outline-show-all= | Show all of the text in the buffer. |
| =C-c C-b= | =outline-backward-same-level= | Move backward to the ARG'th subheading at same level as this one |
| =C-c C-c= | =outline-hide-entry= | Hide the body directly following this heading. |
| =C-c C-d= | =outline-hide-subtree= | Hide everything after this heading at deeper levels |
| =C-c C-e= | =outline-show-entry= | Show the body directly following this heading |
| =C-c C-f= | =outline-forward-same-level= | Move forward to the ARG'th subheading at same level as this one |
| =C-c C-k= | =outline-show-branches= | Show all subheadings of this heading, but not their bodies. |
| =C-c C-l= | =outline-hide-leaves= | Hide the body after this heading and at deeper levels. |
| =C-c C-n= | =outline-next-visible-heading= | Move to the next visible heading line |
| =C-c C-o= | =outline-hide-other= | Hide everything except current body and parent and top-level headings |
| =C-c C-p= | =outline-previous-visible-heading= | Move to the previous heading line |
| =C-c C-q= | =outline-hide-sublevels= | Hide everything but the top LEVELS levels of headers, in whole buffer |
| =C-c C-s= | =outline-show-subtree= | Show everything after this heading at deeper levels |
| =C-c C-t= | =outline-hide-body= | Hide all body lines in buffer, leaving all headings visible |
| =C-c C-u= | =outline-up-heading= | Move to the visible heading line of which the present line is a subheading |
| =C-c C-v= | =outline-move-subtree-down= | Move the current subtree down past ARG headlines of the same level |
| =C-c RET= | =outline-insert-heading= | Insert a new heading at same depth at point. |
| =C-c TAB= | =outline-show-children= | Show all direct subheadings of this heading |

| =C-c C-b= | =eshell-backward-argument= | Move backward ARG arguments |
| =C-c C-e= | =eshell-show-maximum-output= | Put the end of the buffer at the bottom of the window |
| =C-c C-f= | =eshell-forward-argument= | Move forward ARG arguments |
| =C-c C-o= | =eshell-delete-output= | Delete all output from interpreter since last input |
| =C-c C-r= | =eshell-show-output= | Display start of this batch of interpreter output at top of window |
| =C-c C-t= | =eshell-truncate-buffer= | Truncate the buffer to 'eshell-buffer-maximum-lines' |
| =C-c C-u= | =eshell-kill-input= | Kill all text from last stuff output by interpreter to point. |
| =C-c C-w= | =backward-kill-word= | Kill characters backward until encountering the beginning of a word |
| =C-c C-y= | =eshell-repeat-argument= |   (fn &optional ARG) |
| =C-c M-d= | =eshell-toggle-direct-send= |  |
| =C-c M-o= | =eshell-mark-output= | Display start of this batch of interpreter output at top of window |
| =C-c RET= | =eshell-copy-old-input= | Insert after prompt old input at point as new input to be edited. |
| =M-RET= | =eshell-queue-input= | Queue the current input text for execution by Eshell |
| =RET= | =eshell-send-input= | Send the input received to Eshell for parsing and processing |

** VC dir (62)
| Key | Command | What it does |
|---+---+---|
| =% m= | =vc-dir-mark-by-regexp= | Mark all files that match REGEXP |
| =* %= | =vc-dir-mark-by-regexp= | Mark all files that match REGEXP |
| =* r= | =vc-dir-mark-registered-files= | Mark files that are in one of registered states: edited, added or removed. |
| =+= | =vc-pull= | Update the current fileset or branch |
| =<backtab>= | =vc-dir-previous-directory= | Go to the previous directory. |
| =<down-mouse-3>= | =vc-dir-menu= | Popup the VC dir menu |
| =<follow-link>= | =mouse-face= |  |
| === | =vc-diff= | Display diffs between file revisions |
| =@= | =vc-revert= | Revert working copies of the selected fileset to their repository contents |
| =C-<down>= | =vc-dir-next-directory= | Go to the next directory. |
| =C-<up>= | =vc-dir-previous-directory= | Go to the previous directory. |
| =C-c C-c= | =vc-dir-kill-dir-status-process= | Kill the temporary buffer and associated process. |
| =C-k= | =vc-dir-kill-line= | Remove the current line from display. |
| =C-o= | =vc-dir-display-file= | Display the file on the current line, in another window |
| =D= | =vc-root-diff= | Display diffs between VC-controlled whole tree revisions |
| =DEL= | =vc-dir-unmark-file-up= | Move to the previous line and unmark the file. |
| =E == | =vc-diff-outgoing-and-edited= | Report combined diff of outgoing and uncommitted changes to VC fileset |
| =E D= | =vc-root-diff-outgoing-and-edited= | Report combined diff of all outgoing and uncommitted changes |
| =E L= | =vc-root-log-outgoing= | Show log of changes that will be sent with a push to UPSTREAM-LOCATION |
| =G= | =vc-dir-ignore= | Ignore the current file |

| =1..9= | =Info-nth-menu-item= | Go to the node of the Nth menu item |

| =>= | =Info-final-node= | Go to the final node in this file. |
| =?= | =Info-summary= | Display a brief summary of all Info commands. |
| =C-M-i= | =Info-prev-reference= | Move cursor to the previous cross-reference or menu item in the node |
| =DEL= | =Info-scroll-down= | Scroll one screenful back in Info, considering all nodes as one sequence |
| =G= | =Info-goto-node-web= | Use 'browse-url' to go to the gnu.org Web server's version of NODE |
| =H= | =describe-mode= | Display documentation of current major mode and minor modes |
| =I= | =Info-virtual-index= | Show a node with all lines in the index containing a string TOPIC |
| =L= | =Info-history= | Go to a node with a menu of visited nodes. |
| =M-n= | =clone-buffer= | Create and return a twin copy of the current buffer |
| =RET= | =Info-follow-nearest-node= | Follow a node reference near point |
| =S= | =Info-search-case-sensitively= | Search for a regexp case-sensitively. |
| =S-SPC= | =Info-scroll-down= | Scroll one screenful back in Info, considering all nodes as one sequence |
| =SPC= | =Info-scroll-up= | Scroll one screenful forward in Info, considering all nodes as one sequence |
| =T= | =Info-toc= | Go to a node with table of contents of the current Info file |
| =TAB= | =Info-next-reference= | Move cursor to the next cross-reference or menu item in the node |
| =[= | =Info-backward-node= | Go backward one node, considering all nodes as forming one sequence |
| =]= | =Info-forward-node= | Go forward one node, considering all nodes as forming one sequence |
| =^= | =Info-up= | Go to the superior node of this node |

| =c= | =Info-copy-current-node-name= | Put the name of the current Info node into the kill ring |
| =d= | =Info-directory= | Go to the Info directory node. |
| =e= | =end-of-buffer= | Move point to the end of the buffer |
| =f= | =Info-follow-reference= | Follow cross reference named FOOTNOTENAME to the node it refers to |
| =g= | =Info-goto-node= | Go to Info node named NODENAME |
| =h= | =Info-help= | Enter the Info tutorial. |
| =i= | =Info-index= | Look up a string TOPIC in the index for this manual and go to that entry |
| =l= | =Info-history-back= | Go back in the history to the last node visited. |
| =m= | =Info-menu= | Go to the node pointed to by the menu item named (or abbreviated) MENU-ITEM |

| =q= | =quit-window= | Quit WINDOW and bury its buffer |
| =r= | =Info-history-forward= | Go forward in the history of visited nodes. |
| =s= | =Info-search= | Search for REGEXP, starting from point, and select node it's found in |
